Continuing work from thread T-019c72f2-0c78-756c-ab6c-9e1eead1c641. When you lack specific information you can use read_thread to get it.

@docs/feature/embedded-terminal.md @docs/feature/panel-ui-abstraction.md @src/panel/dock.rs @src/panels/mod.rs @src/panels/placeholder.rs @src/model/ui.rs

Other relevant files: src/view/mod.rs, src/view/geometry.rs, src/view/hit_test.rs, src/update/dock.rs

- I just went through a process of reviewing the terminal feature design doc against the current codebase, consulting the oracle for architectural advice, and rewriting the doc based on findings. I want to do the exact same process for the outline panel.

- The key insight from the terminal review was that the dock panel infrastructure is already fully built, which eliminated ~40% of the original terminal work. The outline panel should benefit from the same infrastructure.

- Existing dock/panel infrastructure that's already built:
  - DockLayout with Left/Right/Bottom docks (src/panel/dock.rs)
  - PanelId::Outline already registered, default position is Right dock
  - Dock resize handles, hit testing, focus management all working
  - DockRects in geometry.rs handles layout calculation
  - FocusTarget system for keyboard routing
  - PlaceholderPanel currently renders "Code outline coming soon..." for the outline panel
  - Panel icons defined in src/panels/mod.rs (outline uses "" icon)

- The project uses tree-sitter for syntax highlighting with ~20 languages supported. This is critical for the outline panel since tree-sitter can also provide structural/symbol information for building the outline tree.

- Token uses an Elm Architecture: Message → Update → Command → Render, with winit event loop, fontdue for text rendering, softbuffer for pixel buffer.

- Async operations use std::thread + mpsc channels (syntax worker, fs watcher, PTY workers follow this pattern).

- I told the AI to consult the oracle first to double-check findings before updating the plan. Do the same process: find the design doc (check docs/feature/ and docs/archived/), review it against the codebase, consult oracle, then update.

- When updating design docs, I want them to reflect what infrastructure already exists (mark as done), remove phases that are already complete, and give realistic revised effort estimates.

- The outline panel should show: markdown headings in a collapsible tree, and for code files show classes, methods, properties hierarchically. This is the user's stated requirement.


lets do the same process with the outline panel (should show code section in a collapsible treeview for stuff liek markdown, but for classes or code files it shows stuff like class properties, methods hierarically etc) investigate if this is planned or not and lets review the plan against the codebase to look for outdated stuff or improvemnets to the plan we can do based on current state of codebase

```
The outline panel is currently in a "future/placeholder" state in the codebase. While the infrastructure for the panel (IDs, commands, and keybindings) is fully implemented, the actual structural extraction and tree rendering are not yet present.

The following files contain the relevant implementation and placeholders:

### Panel Implementation & Infrastructure
- [src/panel/dock.rs#L53-L91](file://~/code/token-editor/src/panel/dock.rs#L53-L91) - Defines `PanelId::Outline`, its display name, and default position (Right).
- [src/panels/mod.rs#L12-L34](file://~/code/token-editor/src/panels/mod.rs#L12-L34) - Registers the outline panel in the panel system.
- [src/panels/placeholder.rs#L26-L33](file://~/code/token-editor/src/panels/placeholder.rs#L26-L33) - Provides the "Code outline coming soon..." placeholder message.
- [src/update/dock.rs#L19-L56](file://~/code/token-editor/src/update/dock.rs#L19-L56) - Handles messages for focusing or toggling the outline panel.

### Commands & Keybindings
- [src/commands.rs#L246-L250](file://~/code/token-editor/src/commands.rs#L246-L250) - Command palette definition (`ToggleOutline`, bound to `Cmd+7`).
- [src/keymap/command.rs#L222-L223](file://~/code/token-editor/src/keymap/command.rs#L222-L223) - Command enum for keyboard mapping.
- [src/keymap/defaults.rs#L188](file://~/code/token-editor/src/keymap/defaults.rs#L188) - Default keybinding: `bind(KeyCode::Char('7'), cmd, Command::ToggleOutline)`.

### Tree-Sitter & Query Files
Tree-sitter queries for symbols/tags/outline extraction are **not yet implemented**. The current queries are limited to syntax highlighting:
- [queries/typescript/highlights.scm](file://~/code/token-editor/queries/typescript/highlights.scm) - Example of existing highlight query (no `tags.scm` or `symbols.scm` found).
- [src/syntax/parser.rs#L93-L120](file://~/code/token-editor/src/syntax/parser.rs#L93-L120) - Logic for loading embedded tree-sitter highlight queries.

### Relevant Documentation
- [docs/feature/panel-ui-abstraction.md#L102](file://~/code/token-editor/docs/feature/panel-ui-abstraction.md#L102) - Architectural plan for the unified docking system including the outline panel.
```

```
The syntax highlighting worker thread is implemented in `src/runtime/app.rs` and `src/syntax/parser.rs`.

### Syntax Highlighting Worker Thread
The background thread is spawned in the `App::new` constructor using `std::thread::spawn`. It runs a loop that listens for `SyntaxParseRequest` objects and sends back results via the main application message queue.
- **Worker Loop**: `syntax_worker_loop` in [src/runtime/app.rs#L1465-L1538](file://~/code/token-editor/src/runtime/app.rs#L1465-L1538)
- **Communication**: It uses `std::sync::mpsc` channels. `syntax_tx` sends requests to the worker, and `msg_tx` (the main app loop's sender) sends `Msg::Syntax(SyntaxMsg::ParseCompleted { .. })` back to the main thread.
- **Debouncing**: The main thread manages a `syntax_deadlines` map to debounce requests (defined in `update_syntax` with a 30ms delay).

### Storage in AppModel
The results are stored within the `Document` struct, which is owned by the `EditorArea` inside the `AppModel`.
- **Location**: [src/model/document.rs#L67](file://~/code/token-editor/src/model/document.rs#L67) (`pub syntax_highlights: Option<SyntaxHighlights>`)
- **Update Logic**: When `ParseCompleted` is received, the `update_syntax` handler applies the highlights to the specific document if the revision matches. [src/update/syntax.rs#L83-L125](file://~/code/token-editor/src/update/syntax.rs#L83-L125)

### Tree Caching in ParserState
The `tree-sitter::Tree` is cached per document inside the `ParserState` struct to enable incremental parsing.
- **Cache Structure**: `ParserState` contains `doc_cache: HashMap<DocumentId, DocParseState>`. [src/syntax/parser.rs#L129-L140](file://~/code/token-editor/src/syntax/parser.rs#L129-L140)
- **DocParseState**: Stores the `Tree`, the original `source` string, and the `LanguageId`.
- **Accessibility**: The `ParserState` and its cached `Tree` objects are **not accessible** from outside the worker thread. `ParserState` is initialized locally within the worker loop ([src/runtime/app.rs#L1470](file://~/code/token-editor/src/runtime/app.rs#L1470)) because tree-sitter parsers/trees are `!Sync`. The main thread only receives the processed `SyntaxHighlights` (which is a plain data structure of highlight ranges).
```

```
The sidebar rendering and workspace data models are located in `src/view/mod.rs` and `src/model/workspace.rs` respectively.

### Sidebar Rendering
The file tree is rendered via `render_sidebar` using a recursive `render_node` helper. It handles indentation based on tree depth, shows `+/-` indicators for expansion state, and highlights the selected file.
- [src/view/mod.rs#L908-L1097](file://~/code/token-editor/src/view/mod.rs#L908-L1097) - Main sidebar and recursive tree rendering logic.
- [src/view/mod.rs#L48-L60](file://~/code/token-editor/src/view/mod.rs#L48-L60) - `SidebarRenderContext` containing dimensions, colors, and scroll offsets.
- [src/view/hit_test.rs#L133-L150](file://~/code/token-editor/src/view/hit_test.rs#L133-L150) - Hit testing for sidebar items and the resize handle.

### Workspace & File Tree Model
The data model consists of a `Workspace` that tracks UI state (expanded folders, selection) and a `FileTree` containing a hierarchy of `FileNode`s.
- [src/model/workspace.rs#L560-L582](file://~/code/token-editor/src/model/workspace.rs#L560-L582) - `Workspace` struct tracking `expanded_folders`, `selected_item`, and `scroll_offset`.
- [src/model/workspace.rs#L124-L137](file://~/code/token-editor/src/model/workspace.rs#L124-L137) - `FileNode` struct with `children`, `path`, and `is_dir` flag.
- [src/model/workspace.rs#L216-L220](file://~/code/token-editor/src/model/workspace.rs#L216-L220) - `FileTree` struct representing the workspace root.
- [src/model/workspace.rs#L18-L43](file://~/code/token-editor/src/model/workspace.rs#L18-L43) - `FileExtension` enum used for file type classification and icons.

## TL;DR
Run outline extraction **on the existing syntax worker thread**, reusing its cached tree-sitter `Tree` per document, and return `OutlineData` alongside highlights in the same parse completion message (same revision + same debounce). Extract symbols via **tree-sitter queries** (one `outline.scm` per language, with a generic “range containment” nesting pass), and store the **derived outline per-document**, while keeping **panel UI state** (expanded/selection/scroll) in a separate Outline panel state keyed by `DocumentId`.

---

## Recommended approach (simple path)

### 0) Define the data model (keep it minimal)
Create a small, renderer-friendly structure:

```rust
pub struct OutlineData {
    pub doc_id: DocumentId,
    pub revision: u64,
    pub language: LanguageId,
    pub roots: Vec<OutlineNode>,
}

pub struct OutlineNode {
    pub kind: OutlineKind,          // Heading, Class, Method, Function, …
    pub name: String,
    pub range: OutlineRange,        // start_line, start_col_chars (and optionally end_line)
    pub children: Vec<OutlineNode>,
    pub is_collapsible: bool,       // derived: children non-empty
}

pub struct OutlineRange {
    pub start_line: usize,
    pub start_col: usize,           // char col (not byte col)
    pub end_line: usize,
    pub end_col: usize,             // char col
}
```

For click-to-jump, `start_line/start_col` in **char columns** integrates cleanly with your existing `cursor_to_offset(line, col)` and avoids byte/char mismatch pain later.

---

### 1) Threading: reuse the syntax worker (don’t add a new thread yet)
**Implement outline extraction inside `ParserState` on the syntax worker thread**, immediately after parsing (and optionally after highlights).

**Why this works well in your architecture:**
- `ParserState` and its cached `Tree` live only on that worker thread (by design).
- Outline extraction is “parse-adjacent” derived data, just like highlights.
- You get natural staleness control via `revision` (same pattern as highlights).

**Mechanics:**
- Extend `SyntaxParseRequest` to include a “what outputs do you want?” flag:
  - simplest: always compute both highlights + outline for the active doc
  - slightly better: `want_outline: bool` set when the Outline panel is visible/active
- Extend the worker response (currently returning `SyntaxHighlights`) to also return `Option<OutlineData>`.

---

### 2) Extraction technique: prefer tree-sitter queries + a generic nesting pass
For 20+ languages, **queries scale better than handwritten AST walkers**.

**Recommendation:**
- Add per-language `queries/<lang>/outline.scm` (parallel to `highlights.scm`).
- Compile/cached `Query` per language in `ParserState` like you already do for highlights.
- Run `QueryCursor` to collect “symbol items” (flat list), then build a tree via **range containment**.

**Why queries beat manual `TreeCursor` walking across many languages:**
- Walking AST directly becomes “N bespoke visitors” (high maintenance).
- Queries let you encode language-specific node shapes declaratively.
- You can incrementally expand language coverage: start with Rust/TS/Python/Go/Java, then add more.

**Practical query shape (keep it simple):**
Aim for captures like:
- `@sym.def` = the *node representing the definition scope* (class/function/etc)
- `@sym.name` = identifier node for display text
- `@sym.kind` isn’t a tree-sitter concept; instead, infer kind based on which pattern matched.

Example (conceptual):
```scm
; Rust
(function_item
  name: (identifier) @sym.name) @sym.def

(struct_item
  name: (type_identifier) @sym.name) @sym.def

(impl_item
  type: (type_identifier) @sym.name) @sym.def
```

**Then in Rust code:**
- for each match, determine `OutlineKind` by the pattern index (or by capture name convention like `@kind.function.name`)
- compute `OutlineRange` from `@sym.def` node positions
- derive display name from `@sym.name` node text

**Generic nesting via containment:**
1. Sort all symbols by `(start_byte asc, end_byte desc)` (or by start then longer ranges first).
2. Maintain a stack; for each symbol, pop until it fits inside the top’s range; attach as child; else root.
3. This yields correct hierarchy for:
   - classes/modules containing methods
   - `impl` blocks containing methods (Rust)
   - nested functions where applicable

This avoids needing queries to explicitly encode parent/child relationships.

---

### 3) State placement: store derived outline in `Document`, UI state in a panel state
**Store the actual outline result per-document**, similar to `syntax_highlights`.

Add to `Document`:
```rust
pub outline: Option<OutlineData>;
```

Then create a separate `OutlinePanelState` under `AppModel.ui` (or a dedicated `outline_state` on `AppModel`) for:
- expanded/collapsed nodes
- selected node
- scroll offset inside the panel
- (optional) “follow cursor” toggle

**Key point:** expansion/selection is UI, not document content. Don’t mix it into `Document`.

**Node identity for expansion state (simple, good-enough):**
- Use a best-effort stable key like:
  - `(kind, name, start_line)` or `(kind, name, start_line, start_col)`
- Yes, it can shift during edits; that’s acceptable for v1. If it becomes annoying, revisit later.

---

### 4) Markdown headings: reuse tree-sitter markdown block parse (not regex)
Even though headings *can* be extracted by line-scan, you already have:
- a markdown tree-sitter grammar in the worker
- a cached tree per doc
- logic that already understands fenced code blocks, inline regions, etc.

**Recommendation:** extract headings from the markdown tree.
- Find `heading` nodes.
- Determine level from the heading marker (`#`, `##`, …) or from grammar structure (depends on `tree_sitter_md` node kinds).
- Use heading content node text as the display name.
- Build hierarchy by heading levels (stack by level), not by range containment (both work, but level is the canonical markdown outline model).

This avoids false positives in fenced blocks and handles more markdown variants than a naive scan.

---

### 5) Triggering + debouncing: piggyback on existing syntax parse debounce
**Use the same debounce pipeline as syntax highlighting**. It already exists, is tuned, and is conceptually “parse-derived outputs”.

**Rules of thumb for v1:**
- Trigger outline recompute whenever you trigger syntax parse for the focused document.
- On worker completion:
  - update highlights
  - update outline
  - request redraw (or only redraw if Outline panel is visible)

**Optional optimization (still simple):**
- Only compute outline when:
  - Outline panel is open **and**
  - the parsed document is the focused document (or the document shown in that group)
This keeps CPU down while typing when Outline is hidden.

---

## Rationale and trade-offs
- **Single worker thread** keeps your architecture simple and consistent: “document edit → debounced parse → derived artifacts”.
- **Queries** are the only approach that scales across 20+ languages without turning into a maintenance project.
- **Per-document storage** matches your existing pattern (`syntax_highlights: Option<_>`) and makes “switch tabs” instantaneous.
- **Tree-sitter markdown** is already available and more correct than regex for outline purposes.

Alternative (not recommended for now): a separate “outline worker thread”.
- It only pays off if outline extraction becomes materially expensive and you can’t keep up with typing.
- It introduces additional staleness channels, more message types, and coordination.

---

## Risks and guardrails
1. **Byte vs char column bugs**
   - Guardrail: store outline locations as `(line, col_chars)` only.
   - Convert byte→char in the worker using per-line slices (you already have `byte_to_char_col`).

2. **Queries differ per grammar version**
   - Guardrail: keep outline queries in-repo (like highlights), pinned to the grammars you ship.
   - Start with a handful of languages and expand iteratively.

3. **Hierarchy nesting edge-cases**
   - Range containment can sometimes mis-nest if grammar node ranges are surprising.
   - Guardrail: keep nesting algorithm simple and easy to tweak; allow “flat mode” fallback per language if needed.

4. **UI expansion state churn while typing**
   - Guardrail: accept best-effort keys initially; if users complain, revisit identity strategy.

---

## When to consider the advanced path
Revisit a more complex design if:
- outline extraction causes noticeable input latency (e.g., worker can’t keep up at 60fps typing),
- you want outlines for *many open docs simultaneously* (not just the focused one),
- or you want richer symbol semantics (signatures, visibility, docstrings, icons by modifier).

---

## Optional advanced path (only if needed later)
- Split worker outputs into stages: parse → highlight, parse → outline, scheduled with different debounce intervals (e.g., highlights at 50ms, outline at 150–250ms while typing).
- Maintain an LRU cache of outline results per doc and only update for “active/visible” docs.
- Add a lightweight symbol identity system (e.g., “path” = `Module::Class::method`, plus nearest enclosing container names from the containment stack).

---

### Effort / scope signal
- **M (1–3h)** to wire the pipeline end-to-end for Markdown + 1 language (e.g., Rust) and render a basic tree with click-to-jump.
- **L (1–2d)** to add robust queries for your top ~6 languages and polish nesting + UI state (expand/collapse persistence, selection, keyboard nav).
```

Hierarchy is built by **range containment** — a generic algorithm that works across all languages.\n\n### Query Format\n\nEach language gets an `outline.scm` with patterns that capture `@sym.def` (the full scope node) and `@sym.name` (the display name). The `OutlineKind` is inferred from the pattern index.\n\nExample — `queries/rust/outline.scm`:\n```scm\n; Functions\n(function_item\n  name: (identifier) @sym.name) @sym.def\n\n; Structs\n(struct_item\n  name: (type_identifier) @sym.name) @sym.def\n\n; Enums\n(enum_item\n  name: (type_identifier) @sym.name) @sym.def\n\n; Enum variants\n(enum_variant\n  name: (identifier) @sym.name) @sym.def\n\n; Impl blocks\n(impl_item\n  type: (type_identifier) @sym.name) @sym.def\n\n; Traits\n(trait_item\n  name: (type_identifier) @sym.name) @sym.def\n\n; Constants\n(const_item\n `queries/markdown/outline.scm`:\n```scm\n(atx_heading) @sym.def\n```\n\n### Range Containment Algorithm\n\nAfter collecting a flat list of symbols from query matches, build hierarchy:\n\n```rust\nfn build_tree(mut symbols: Vec<FlatSymbol>) -> Vec<OutlineNode> {\n    // Sort by (start_byte asc, end_byte desc) — parents before children\n    symbols.sort_by_key(|s| (s.start_byte, std::cmp::Reverse(s.end_byte)));\n\n    let mut roots: Vec<OutlineNode> = Vec::new();\n    let mut stack: Vec<OutlineNode> = Vec::new();\n\n    for sym in symbols {\n        let node = OutlineNode {\n            kind: sym.kind,\n            name: sym.name,\n            range: sym.range,\n            children: Vec::new(),\n        };\n\n        // Pop items off stack that don't contain this node\n        while let Some(top) = stack.last() {\n            if top.range.end_line >= node.range.start_line\n               && top.range.start_line <= node.range.start_line {\n                break; // current top contains this node\n            }\n            let finished = stack.pop().unwrap();\n            if let Some(parent) = stack.last_mut() {\n                parent.children.push(finished);\n            } else {\n                roots.push(finished);\n            }\n        }\n\n        stack.push(node);\n    }\n\n    // Flush remaining stack\n    while let Some(finished) = stack.pop() {\n        if let Some(parent) = stack.last_mut() {\n            parent.children.push(finished);\n        } else {\n            roots.push(finished);\n        }\n    }\n\n    roots\n}\n```\n\n### Markdown Heading Hierarchy\n\nMarkdown headings use level-based nesting rather than range containment, since headings don't have explicit scope ranges:\n\n```rust\nfn build_heading_tree(headings: Vec<(u8, String, usize)>) -> Vec<OutlineNode> {\n    // Stack-based: H1 contains H2, H2 contains H3, etc.\n    // A heading at level N closes all open headings at level >= N\n}\n```\n\n### Language Support Priority\n\n| Phase | Languages | Notes |\n|-------|-----------|-------|\n| MVP | Markdown, Rust | Core languages for dogfooding |\n| Fast-follow | TypeScript, JavaScript, Python | Most popular |\n| Extended | Go, Java, C, C++, PHP | Remaining supported languages |\n| Fallback | All others | No outline (panel shows \"No outline available\") |\n\n---\n\n## Messages\n\n```rust\n// In src/messages.rs — add to existing Msg enum\n\npub enum OutlineMsg {\n    /// Click on outline node → jump to symbol\n    JumpToSymbol { line: usize, col: usize },\n\n    /// Toggle expand/collapse of a node\n    ToggleNode { line: usize, name: String },\n\n    /// Keyboard navigation within outline panel\n    SelectPrevious,\n    SelectNext,\n\n    /// Expand selected node (ArrowRight)\n Outline extraction from tree-sitter parse trees\n//!\n//! Walks the tree-sitter AST to extract structural symbols.\n//! Runs on the syntax worker thread.\n\nuse tree_sitter::{Node, Tree};\n\nuse super::{OutlineData, OutlineKind, OutlineNode, OutlineRange};\nuse crate::syntax::LanguageId;\n\n/// Extract outline from a tree-sitter parse tree\npub fn extract_outline(tree: &Tree, source: &str, language: LanguageId, revision: u64) -> OutlineData {\n    let root = tree.root_node();\n\n    let nodes = match language {\n        LanguageId::Markdown => extract_markdown_headings(root, source),\n        LanguageId::Rust => {\n            let flat = extract_rust_symbols(root, source);\n            build_tree_by_containment(flat)\n        }\n        LanguageId::TypeScript | LanguageId::Tsx | LanguageId::JavaScript => {\n            let flat = extract_js_ts_symbols(root, source);\n            build_tree_by_containment(flat)\n        }\n        LanguageId::Python => {\n            let flat = extract_python_symbols(root, source);\n            build_tree_by_containment(flat)\n        }\n        LanguageId::Go => {\n            let flat = extract_go_symbols(root, source);\n            build_tree_by_containment(flat)\n        }\n        LanguageId::Java => {\n            let flat = extract_java_symbols(root, source);\n            build_tree_by_containment(flat)\n        }\n        LanguageId::Php => {\n            let flat = extract_php_symbols(root, source);\n            build_tree_by_containment(flat)\n        }\n        LanguageId::C | LanguageId::Cpp => {\n            let flat = extract_c_cpp_symbols(root, source, language);\n            build_tree_by_containment(flat)\n        }\n        _ => Vec::new(),\n    };\n\n    OutlineData {\n        revision,\n        roots: nodes,\n    }\n}\n\n// =============================================================================\n// Flat symbol for pre-nesting\n// =============================================================================\n\nstruct FlatSymbol {\n    kind: OutlineKind,\n    name: String,\n    start_byte: usize,\n    end_byte: usize,\n    range: OutlineRange,\n}\n\nfn node_range(node: &Node) -> OutlineRange {\n    let start = node.start_position();\n    let end = node.end_position();\n    OutlineRange {\n        start_line: start.row,\n        start_col: start.column,\n        end_line: end.row,\n        end_col: end.column,\n    }\n}\n\nfn node_name<'a>(node: &Node, source: &'a str) -> Option<&'a str> {\n    node.utf8_text(source.as_bytes()).ok()\n}\n\nfn child_by_field<'a>(node: &Node<'a>, field: &str) -> Option<Node<'a>> {\n    node.child_by_field_name(field)\n}\n\nfn flat_sym(kind: OutlineKind, name: &str, node: &Node) -> FlatSymbol {\n    FlatSymbol {\n        kind,\n        name: name.to_string(),\n        start_byte: node.start_byte(),\n        end_byte: node.end_byte(),\n        range: node_range(node),\n    }\n}\n\n// =============================================================================\n// Markdown: level-based heading hierarchy\n// =============================================================================\n\nfn extract_markdown_headings(root: Node, source: &str) -> Vec<OutlineNode> {\n    let mut headings: Vec<(u8, String, OutlineRange)> = Vec::new();\n\n    // Walk top-level children looking for atx_heading nodes\n    let mut cursor = root.walk();\n    for child in root.children(&mut cursor) {\n        if child.kind() == \"atx_heading\" || child.kind() == \"setext_heading\" {\n            if let Some((level, text)) = parse_heading(&child, source) {\n                headings.push((level, text, node_range(&child)));\n            }\n        }\n        // Also check inside section nodes (tree-sitter-markdown wraps in sections)\n        if child.kind() == \"section\" {\n            collect_headings_recursive(child, source, &mut headings);\n        }\n    }\n\n    build_heading_tree(headings)\n}\n\nfn collect_headings_recursive(node: Node, source: &str, headings: &mut Vec<(u8, String, OutlineRange)>) {\n    let mut cursor = node.walk();\n    for child in node.children(&mut cursor) {\n        if child.kind() == \"atx_heading\" || child.kind() == \"setext_heading\" {\n            if let Some((level, text)) = parse_heading(&child, source) {\n                headings.push((level, text, node_range(&child)));\n            }\n        }\n        if child.kind() == \"section\" {\n            collect_headings_recursive(child, source, headings);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfn parse_heading(node: &Node, source: &str) -> Option<(u8, String)> {\n    if node.kind() == \"atx_heading\" {\n        // Count # markers from the atx_h*_marker child\n        let mut level = 1u8;\n        let mut text = String::new();\n\n        let mut cursor = node.walk();\n        for child in node.children(&mut cursor) {\n            let kind = child.kind();\n            if kind.starts_with(\"atx_h\") && kind.ends_with(\"_marker\") {\n                // Parse level from marker text (count '#' chars)\n                if let Some(marker_text) = node_name(&child, source) {\n                    level = marker_text.chars().filter(|c| *c == '#').count().min(6) as u8;\n                }\n            } else if kind == \"inline\" || kind == \"heading_content\" {\n                if let Some(t) = node_name(&child, source) {\n                    text = t.trim().to_string();\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // If we didn't get inline text, try full heading text minus markers\n        if text.is_empty() {\n            if let Some(full) = node_name(node, source) {\n                text = full.trim_start_matches('#').trim().to_string();\n            }\n        }\n\n        if !text.is_empty() {\n            return Some((level, text));\n        }\n    }\n\n    if node.kind() == \"setext_heading\" {\n        // Setext headings: text followed by === or ---\n        let mut cursor = node.walk();\n        let mut text = String::new();\n        let mut level = 2u8; // default to h2\n\n        for child in node.children(&mut cursor) {\n            let kind = child.kind();\n            if kind == \"heading_content\" || kind == \"paragraph\" || kind == \"inline\" {\n                if let Some(t) = node_name(&child, source) {\n                    text = t.trim().to_string();\n                }\n            } else if kind == \"setext_h1_underline\" {\n                level = 1;\n            } else if kind == \"setext_h2_underline\" {\n                level = 2;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if !text.is_empty() {\n            return Some((level, text));\n        }\n    }\n\n    None\n}\n\nfn build_heading_tree(headings: Vec<(u8, String, OutlineRange)>) -> Vec<OutlineNode> {\n    let mut roots: Vec<OutlineNode> = Vec::new();\n    // Stack: (level, node) — each entry is a heading being built\n    let mut stack: Vec<(u8, OutlineNode)> = Vec::new();\n\n    for (level, text, range) in headings {\n        let node = OutlineNode {\n            kind: OutlineKind::Heading { level },\n            name: text,\n            range,\n            children: Vec::new(),\n        };\n\n        // Pop everything at same level or deeper\n        while let Some((top_level, _)) = stack.last() {\n            if *top_level >= level {\n                let (_, finished) = stack.pop().unwrap();\n                if let Some((_, parent)) = stack.last_mut() {\n                    parent.children.push(finished);\n                } else {\n                    roots.push(finished);\n                }\n            } else {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        stack.push((level, node));\n    }\n\n    // Flush remaining stack\n    while let Some((_, finished)) = stack.pop() {\n        if let Some((_, parent)) = stack.last_mut() {\n            parent.children.push(finished);\n        } else {\n            roots.push(finished);\n        }\n    }\n\n    roots\n}\n\n// =============================================================================\n// Range-containment nesting (for code languages)\n// =============================================================================\n\nfn build_tree_by_containment(mut symbols: Vec<FlatSymbol>) -> Vec<OutlineNode> {\n    if symbols.is_empty() {\n        return Vec::new();\n    }\n\n    // Sort by (start_byte asc, end_byte desc) — parents before children\n    symbols.sort_by(|a, b| {\n        a.start_byte.cmp(&b.start_byte)\n            .then(b.end_byte.cmp(&a.end_byte))\n    });\n\n    let mut roots: Vec<OutlineNode> = Vec::new();\n    // Stack: (end_byte, node)\n    let mut stack: Vec<(usize, OutlineNode)> = Vec::new();\n\n    for sym in symbols {\n        let node = OutlineNode {\n            kind: sym.kind,\n            name: sym.name,\n            range: sym.range,\n            children: Vec::new(),\n        };\n\n        // Pop items that don't contain this symbol\n        while let Some((top_end, _)) = stack.last() {\n            if *top_end <= sym.start_byte {\n                let (_, finished) = stack.pop().unwrap();\n                if let Some((_, parent)) = stack.last_mut() {\n                    parent.children.push(finished);\n                } else {\n                    roots.push(finished);\n                }\n            } else {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        stack.push((sym.end_byte, node));\n    }\n\n    // Flush remaining stack\n    while let Some((_, finished)) = stack.pop() {\n        if let Some((_, parent)) = stack.last_mut() {\n            parent.children.push(finished);\n        } else {\n            roots.push(finished);\n        }\n    }\n\n    roots\n}\n\n// =============================================================================\n// Rust symbol extraction\n// =============================================================================\n\nfn extract_rust_symbols(root: Node, source: &str) -> Vec<FlatSymbol> {\n    let mut symbols = Vec::new();\n    collect_rust_symbols(root, source, &mut symbols);\n    symbols\n}\n\nfn collect_rust_symbols(node: Node, source: &str, symbols: &mut Vec<FlatSymbol>) {\n    match node.kind() {\n        \"function_item\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Function, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"struct_item\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Struct, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"enum_item\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Enum, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"enum_variant\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::EnumVariant, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"impl_item\" => {\n            // Get the type being implemented\n            if let Some(type_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"type\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&type_node, source) {\n                    // Check for trait impl: impl Trait for Type\n                    let label = if let Some(trait_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"trait\") {\n                        if let Some(trait_name) = node_name(&trait_node, source) {\n                            format!(\"{} for {}\", trait_name, name)\n                        } else {\n                            name.to_string()\n                        }\n                    } else {\n                        name.to_string()\n                    };\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Impl, &label, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"trait_item\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Trait, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"const_item\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Constant, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"static_item\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Constant, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"mod_item\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Module, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"field_declaration\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Field, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        _ => {}\n    }\n\n    // Recurse into children\n    let mut cursor = node.walk();\n    for child in node.children(&mut cursor) {\n        collect_rust_symbols(child, source, symbols);\n    }\n}\n\n// =============================================================================\n// TypeScript/JavaScript symbol extraction\n// =============================================================================\n\nfn extract_js_ts_symbols(root: Node, source: &str) -> Vec<FlatSymbol> {\n    let mut symbols = Vec::new();\n    collect_js_ts_symbols(root, source, &mut symbols);\n    symbols\n}\n\nfn collect_js_ts_symbols(node: Node, source: &str, symbols: &mut Vec<FlatSymbol>) {\n    match node.kind() {\n        \"function_declaration\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Function, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"class_declaration\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Class, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"method_definition\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Method, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"interface_declaration\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Interface, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"type_alias_declaration\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Interface, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"enum_declaration\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Enum, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"public_field_definition\" | \"property_signature\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Property, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"lexical_declaration\" | \"variable_declaration\" => {\n            // Only top-level const/let/var declarations\n            let mut cursor = node.walk();\n            for child in node.children(&mut cursor) {\n                if child.kind() == \"variable_declarator\" {\n                    if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&child, \"name\") {\n                        if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                            // Check if value is an arrow function or function expression\n                            let kind = if child.child_by_field_name(\"value\")\n                                .map(|v| v.kind() == \"arrow_function\" || v.kind() == \"function\")\n                                .unwrap_or(false)\n                            {\n                                OutlineKind::Function\n                            } else {\n                                OutlineKind::Constant\n                            };\n                            symbols.push(flat_sym(kind, name, &node));\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"export_statement\" => {\n            // Recurse into the declaration within the export\n            let mut cursor = node.walk();\n            for child in node.children(&mut cursor) {\n                if child.kind() != \"export_statement\" {\n                    collect_js_ts_symbols(child, source, symbols);\n                }\n            }\n            return; // Don't recurse again below\n        }\n        _ => {}\n    }\n\n    // Recurse into children (but skip deep recursion into function/class bodies for top-level-only items)\n    let mut cursor = node.walk();\n    for child in node.children(&mut cursor) {\n        collect_js_ts_symbols(child, source, symbols);\n    }\n}\n\n// =============================================================================\n// Python symbol extraction\n// =============================================================================\n\nfn extract_python_symbols(root: Node, source: &str) -> Vec<FlatSymbol> {\n    let mut symbols = Vec::new();\n \"function_definition\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Function, name, &node));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \"class_definition\" => {\n            if let Some(name_node) = child_by_field(&node, child_by_field(&node, \"name\") {\n                if let Some(name) = node_name(&name_node, source) {\n                    symbols.push(flat_sym(OutlineKind::Interface, name, &node));\n // Draw kind label + name\n+                let fg = if is_selected { selection_fg } else { text_color };\n+                let label = node.kind.label();\n+\n+                // Draw label in dimmer color, then name\n+                let label_color = if is_selected { selection_fg } else { folder_icon_color };\n+                painter.draw(frame, text_x, text_y, label, label_color);\n+\n+                let name_x = text_x + (label.len() + 1) * painter.char_width() as usize;\n+\n+                // Truncate name if needed\n+                let right_padding = 8;\n+                let available = (rect.x as usize + rect.width as usize).saturating_sub(name_x + right_padding);\n+                let char_w = painter.char_width() as usize;\n+                let max_chars = if char_w > 0 { available / char_w } else { 80 };\n+\n+                if node.name.len() > max_chars && max_chars > 3 {\n+                    let truncated = &node.name[..max_chars.saturating_sub(1)];\n+                    let mut display = String::with_capacity(truncated.len() + 3);\n+                    display.push_str(truncated);\n+                    display.push('…');\n+                    painter.draw(frame, name_x, text_y, &display, fg);\n+                } else {\n+                    painter.draw(frame, name_x, text_y, &node.name, fg);\n+                }\n+\n+                *y += row_height;\n+            }\n+\n+            // Recurse into children if expanded\n+            if node.is_collapsible() && !outline_panel.is_collapsed(node) {\n+                for child in &node.children {\n+                    render_outline_node(\n+                        frame,\n+                        painter,\n+                        child,\n+                        outline_panel,\n+                        y,\n+                        visible_index,\n+                        depth + 1,\n+                        scroll_offset,\n+                        selected_index,\n+                        rect,\n+                        max_y,\n+                        row_height,\n+                        indent,\n+                        text_color,\n+                        selection_bg,\n+                        selection_fg,\n+                        folder_icon_color,\n+                    );\n+                }\n+            }\n+        }\n+\n+        for node in &outline.roots {\n+            render_outline_node(\n+                frame,\n+                painter,\n+                node,\n+                &model.outline_panel,\n+                &mut y,\n+                &mut visible_index,\n+                0,\n+                scroll_offset,\n+                selected_index,\n+                &rect,\n+                max_y,\n+                row_height,\n+                indent,\n+                text_color,\n+                selection_bg,\n+                selection_fg,\n+                folder_icon_color,\n+            );\n+            if visible_index >= scroll_offset && y >= max_y {\n+                break;\n+            }\n+        }\n+    }\n+\n     /// Render the gutter (line numbers and border) for an editor group.\n     ///\n Outline panel update handlers\n\nuse crate::commands::Cmd;\nuse crate::messages::OutlineMsg;\nuse crate::model::{AppModel, FocusTarget};\nuse crate::outline::OutlineNode;\n\n/// Count total visible items in the outline tree (for navigation bounds)\nfn count_visible_items(\n    nodes: &[OutlineNode],\n    panel: &crate::model::OutlinePanelState,\n) -> usize {\n    let mut count = 0;\n    for node in nodes {\n        count += 1;\n        if node.is_collapsible() && !panel.is_collapsed(node) {\n            count += count_visible_items(&node.children, panel);\n        }\n    }\n    count\n}\n\n/// Get the node at a given flattened visible index\nfn node_at_index<'a>(\n    nodes: &'a [OutlineNode],\n    panel: &crate::model::OutlinePanelState,\n    target: usize,\n    current: &mut usize,\n) -> Option<&'a OutlineNode> {\n    for node in nodes {\n        if *current == target {\n            return Some(node);\n        }\n        *current += 1;\n        if node.is_collapsible() && !panel.is_collapsed(node) {\n            if let Some(found) = node_at_index(&node.children, panel, target, current) {\n                return Some(found);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    None\n}\n\n/// Handle outline panel messages\npub fn update_outline(model: &mut AppModel, msg: OutlineMsg) -> Option<Cmd> {\n    match msg {\n        OutlineMsg::JumpToSymbol { line, col } => {\n            // Move cursor to the symbol and focus the editor\n            let editor = model.editor_mut();\n            editor.cursors[0].line = line;\n            editor.cursors[0].column = col;\n            editor.cursors[0].desired_column = None;\n            editor.clear_selection();\n            model.ensure_cursor_visible();\n            model.ui.focus_editor();\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        OutlineMsg::ToggleNode { line, name } => {\n            // Find node by line+name and toggle\n            let outline = model\n                .editor_area\n                .focused_document()\n                .and_then(|doc| doc.outline.as_ref());\n\n            if let Some(outline) = outline {\n                // Find the matching node\n                fn find_node<'a>(\n                    nodes: &'a [OutlineNode],\n                    line: usize,\n                    name: &str,\n                ) -> Option<&'a OutlineNode> {\n                    for node in nodes {\n                        if node.range.start_line == line && node.name == name {\n                            return Some(node);\n                        }\n                        if let Some(found) = find_node(&node.children, line, name) {\n                            return Some(found);\n                        }\n                    }\n                    None\n                }\n\n                if let Some(node) = find_node(&outline.roots, line, &name) {\n                    model.outline_panel.toggle_collapsed(node);\n                }\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        OutlineMsg::SelectPrevious => {\n            if let Some(idx) = model.outline_panel.selected_index {\n                if idx > 0 {\n                    model.outline_panel.selected_index = Some(idx - 1);\n                }\n            } else {\n                model.outline_panel.selected_index = Some(0);\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        OutlineMsg::SelectNext => {\n            let outline = model\n                .editor_area\n                .focused_document()\n                .and_then(|doc| doc.outline.as_ref());\n\n            if let Some(outline) = outline {\n                let total = count_visible_items(&outline.roots, &model.outline_panel);\n                if let Some(idx) = model.outline_panel.selected_index {\n                    if idx + 1 < total {\n                        model.outline_panel.selected_index = Some(idx + 1);\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    model.outline_panel.selected_index = Some(0);\n                }\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        OutlineMsg::ExpandSelected => {\n            if let Some(idx) = model.outline_panel.selected_index {\n                let outline = model\n                    .editor_area\n                    .focused_document()\n                    .and_then(|doc| doc.outline.as_ref());\n\n                if let Some(outline) = outline {\n                    let mut current = 0;\n                    if let Some(node) =\n                        node_at_index(&outline.roots, &model.outline_panel, idx, &mut current)\n                    {\n                        if node.is_collapsible() {\n                            let key = crate::model::OutlinePanelState::node_key(node);\n                            model.outline_panel.collapsed.remove(&key);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        OutlineMsg::CollapseSelected => {\n            if let Some(idx) = model.outline_panel.selected_index {\n                let outline = model\n                    .editor_area\n                    .focused_document()\n                    .and_then(|doc| doc.outline.as_ref());\n\n                if let Some(outline) = outline {\n                    let mut current = 0;\n                    if let Some(node) =\n                        node_at_index(&outline.roots, &model.outline_panel, idx, &mut current)\n                    {\n                        if node.is_collapsible() {\n                            let key = crate::model::OutlinePanelState::node_key(node);\n                            model.outline_panel.collapsed.insert(key);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        OutlineMsg::OpenSelected => {\n            if let Some(idx) = model.outline_panel.selected_index {\n                let outline = model\n                    .editor_area\n                    .focused_document()\n                    .and_then(|doc| doc.outline.as_ref());\n\n                if let Some(outline) = outline {\n                    let mut current = 0;\n                    if let Some(node) =\n                        node_at_index(&outline.roots, &model.outline_panel, idx, &mut current)\n                    {\n                        let line = node.range.start_line;\n                        let col = node.range.start_col;\n                        // Jump to symbol and focus editor\n                        let editor = model.editor_mut();\n                        editor.cursors[0].line = line;\n                        editor.cursors[0].column = col;\n                        editor.cursors[0].desired_column = None;\n                        editor.clear_selection();\n                        model.ensure_cursor_visible();\n                        model.ui.focus_editor();\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        OutlineMsg::Scroll { lines } => {\n            let offset = model.outline_panel.scroll_offset;\n            if lines < 0 {\n                model.outline_panel.scroll_offset = offset.saturating_sub((-lines) as usize);\n            } else {\n                model.outline_panel.scroll_offset = offset + lines as usize;\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n ## Assistant

All tests pass, lint clean. Here's what was implemented:

### Summary

**Phases 1-3 of the outline panel are now implemented:**

#### New files created:
- **`src/outline/mod.rs`** — `OutlineData`, `OutlineNode`, `OutlineKind`, `OutlineRange` data structures
- **`src/outline/extract.rs`** — Symbol extraction via tree-sitter AST walking for 8 language families (Rust, TS/JS, Python, Go, Java, PHP, C/C++) + markdown heading hierarchy with 3 unit tests
- **`src/update/outline.rs`** — `OutlineMsg` handler (jump-to-symbol, navigate, expand/collapse, scroll)
- **`docs/feature/code-outline.md`** — Full design doc

#### Files modified:
- **`src/model/document.rs`** — Added `outline: Option<OutlineData>` per document
- **`src/model/ui.rs`** — Added `OutlinePanelState` (collapse tracking, selection, scroll)
- **`src/model/mod.rs`** — Added `outline_panel` to `AppModel`
- **`src/messages.rs`** — Added `OutlineMsg` enum + `Msg::Outline` variant + `outline` field on `ParseCompleted`
- **`src/syntax/parser.rs`** — Added `get_cached_tree()` accessor
- **`src/runtime/app.rs`** — Syntax worker extracts outline alongside highlights
- **`src/update/syntax.rs`** — Stores outline on document from `ParseCompleted`
- **`src/update/mod.rs`** — Wired `Msg::Outline` dispatch
- **`src/view/mod.rs`** — `render_dock` dispatches to `render_outline_panel` for the outline, showing a tree with expand/collapse indicators, kind labels, and symbol names
- **`src/lib.rs`** — Registered `outline` module
- Various bin/test/bench files — Added `outline_panel` field

#### What works now:
- Open a Rust/TS/Python/etc file → outline is extracted on the syntax worker thread alongside highlights
- Press `Cmd+7` → outline panel opens in right dock showing symbol tree
- Symbols nested by range containment (methods inside impl/class)
- Markdown headings nested by level (`# > ## > ###`)
- "No outline available" shown for unsupported languages
